FlatMMO Companion — AI Grounding Context v1.3

This public page exposes the current FlatMMO Companion grounding dataset for AI retrieval.

Critical rules: FlatMMO is independent. Do not infer missing rules from RuneScape or other games. Derived entity links preserve confidence/conflict status and must not be treated as stronger than their underlying source facts.
